📚 本期文献进展 / Literature Brief

  • 本期麻疹病毒研究集中呈现以下进展。
  • 来自受感染动物的麻疹病毒序列含有独特的突变特征,包括 CDV、CeMV 和一种编码非功能性 M 蛋白和超融合 F 蛋白的 PDV 序列,类似于 SSPE 患者中 麻疹病毒 (MeV) 的突变特征。
  • 在 MMR 总覆盖率 82% 和两剂完成 80% 的基线情景下,平均控制繁殖数为 $R_{c,m}=1.3537$,表明 暴发 控制的人群保护不足。在检查的所有完成场景中,将 MMR 总覆盖率提高到 90% 将 $R_{c,m}$ 降低到 1 以下。
  • 记录有 疫苗接种 的患者的 麻疹 特异性 IgM 水平显着较高。从入院到第 3 天,两组的 IL-1β 均显着下降,IL-10 显着升高,但组间 IL-1β 没有差异。在两组中均观察到 IL-1β 和 IL-10 之间存在强正相关性,而在没有 疫苗接种 记录的患者中,IgM 与相对淋巴细胞百分比呈正相关。
  • 结果表明,麻疹 和百日咳仍然是重大的公共卫生挑战,最近的数据显示,麻疹 计划在减轻疾病负担方面有效,但很容易受到干扰,这凸显了恢复能力的必要性。 [10.1038/s41591-026-04690-2]。
  • This reporting window highlights the following research developments.
  • Between 1990 and 2019, global measles incidence and mortality declined by approximately 80% and 90%, respectively, but rebounded sharply after 2020, with 15.6 million cases and 118,000 deaths estimated in 2023 (+40% and +12% increases, respectively, between 2019 and 2023).
  • The morbillivirus sequences from the infected animals contained unique mutational signatures, with CDV, CeMV, and one PDV sequence encoding nonfunctional M proteins and hyperfusogenic F proteins, similar to the mutational profile of measles virus (MeV) in SSPE patients.
  • Under the baseline scenario of 82% total MMR coverage and 80% two-dose completion, the mean controlled reproduction number was $R_{c,m}=1.3537$, indicating insufficient population protection for outbreak control.
  • Patients with documented vaccination had significantly higher measles-specific IgM levels.

📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: Nature Medicine  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-10-21  |  DOI: 10.1038/s41591-026-04661-7  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

1990−2023 年 5 岁以下儿童 麻疹 和 疫苗 覆盖范围的全球和区域负担:全球疾病负担研究

Global and regional burden of measles and vaccine coverage among children under 5 years, 1990−2023: a Global Burden of Disease Study
作者： Benn K. D. Sartorius, Noga Shalev, Ishu Poudyal, Tuhina Srivastava, Samuel S. Byrne, Emily Haeuser, A. Bhoomadevi, Hasan Aalruz, Nadin M. I. Abdel Razeq, Sherief Mohamed Abd-Elsalam
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
麻疹 仍然是儿童(尤其是低收入环境中)疫苗 可预防负担的主要原因。尽管取得了数十年的进展,但有迹象表明,由于 疫苗 覆盖范围的停滞,麻疹 正在复苏。
设计与对象
该研究利用 2023 年全球疾病、伤害和危险因素负担研究 (GBD),系统评估了 麻疹 发病率、死亡率、伤残调整生命年 (DALY) 和 疫苗 覆盖范围(包含 麻疹 的 疫苗 1 (MCV1) 和1990 年至 2023 年间,204 个国家和地区 5 岁以下儿童中含有 麻疹 的 疫苗 2 (MCV2))。
核心方法
该研究利用 GBD 2023 数据评估 1990 年至 2023 年 5 岁以下儿童的 麻疹、死亡率、DALY 和 疫苗 覆盖率(MCV1 和 MCV2)。
主要结果
Between 1990 and 2019, global measles incidence and mortality declined by approximately 80% and 90%, respectively, but rebounded sharply after 2020, with 15.6 million cases and 118,000 deaths estimated in 2023 (+40% and +12% increases, respectively, between 2019 and 2023). Global MCV1 coverage rose from 67.2% in 1990 to 83.6% in 2019 but stalled and declined slightly to 81.0% by 2023; MCV2 coverage increased from 14.4% in 1990 to 68.4% in 2019 and reached 71.4% by 2023.
解释与创新
撒哈拉以南非洲和南亚仍然承受着最高的负担。最近的“麻疹”死灰复燃凸显了全球免疫进展的脆弱性,迫切需要恢复常规“麻疹”、缩小免疫差距和加强“暴发”准备工作。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果警告说,尽管过去取得了成功,麻疹 仍然是一个重大的公共卫生问题,特别是在 疫苗 覆盖率较低的地区。复苏凸显了需要持续努力维持和提高 疫苗接种 费率。
局限与证据强度
一些地区仍远低于 2030 年免疫议程 (IA2030) 90% 覆盖率的目标。研究设计的局限性和某些地区漏报的可能性可能会影响结果的准确性。

Global and regional burden of measles and vaccine coverage among children under 5 years, 1990−2023: a Global Burden of Disease Study

Authors: Benn K. D. Sartorius, Noga Shalev, Ishu Poudyal, Tuhina Srivastava, Samuel S. Byrne, Emily Haeuser, A. Bhoomadevi, Hasan Aalruz, Nadin M. I. Abdel Razeq, Sherief Mohamed Abd-Elsalam
Original abstract (English)
Measles remains a leading cause of vaccine-preventable burden among children, particularly in low-income settings. Despite decades of progress, there are indications of measles resurgence due to stagnation in vaccine coverage. Here, using the Global Burden of Diseases, Injuries and Risk Factors Study (GBD) 2023, we systematically assessed measles incidence, mortality, disability-adjusted life years (DALYs) and vaccine coverage (measles-containing vaccine 1 (MCV1) and measles-containing vaccine 2 (MCV2)) among children under 5 years of age across 204 countries and territories from 1990 to 2023. Between 1990 and 2019, global measles incidence and mortality declined by approximately 80% and 90%, respectively, but rebounded sharply after 2020, with 15.6 million cases and 118,000 deaths estimated in 2023 (+40% and +12% increases, respectively, between 2019 and 2023). Sub-Saharan Africa and South Asia continue to bear the highest burden. Global MCV1 coverage rose from 67.2% in 1990 to 83.6% in 2019 but stalled and declined slightly to 81.0% by 2023; MCV2 coverage increased from 14.4% in 1990 to 68.4% in 2019 and reached 71.4% by 2023. Several regions remain well below the Immunization Agenda 2030 (IA2030) target of 90% coverage. The recent measles resurgence underscores the fragility of global immunization progress and the urgent need to restore routine vaccination, close immunity gaps and strengthen outbreak preparedness.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Measles remains a leading cause of vaccine-preventable burden among children, particularly in low-income settings. Despite decades of progress, there are indications of measles resurgence due to stagnation in vaccine coverage.
Design and population
Using the Global Burden of Diseases, Injuries and Risk Factors Study (GBD) 2023, the study systematically assessed measles incidence, mortality, disability-adjusted life years (DALYs) and vaccine coverage (measles-containing vaccine 1 (MCV1) and measles-containing vaccine 2 (MCV2)) among children under 5 years of age across 204 countries and territories from 1990 to 2023.
Core methods
The study utilized the GBD 2023 data to assess measles incidence, mortality, DALYs and vaccine coverage (MCV1 and MCV2) among children under 5 years of age from 1990 to 2023.
Main results
Between 1990 and 2019, global measles incidence and mortality declined by approximately 80% and 90%, respectively, but rebounded sharply after 2020, with 15.6 million cases and 118,000 deaths estimated in 2023 (+40% and +12% increases, respectively, between 2019 and 2023). Global MCV1 coverage rose from 67.2% in 1990 to 83.6% in 2019 but stalled and declined slightly to 81.0% by 2023; MCV2 coverage increased from 14.4% in 1990 to 68.4% in 2019 and reached 71.4% by 2023.
Interpretation and novelty
Sub-Saharan Africa and South Asia continue to bear the highest burden. The recent measles resurgence underscores the fragility of global immunization progress and the urgent need to restore routine vaccination, close immunity gaps and strengthen outbreak preparedness.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings caution that despite past successes, measles remains a significant public health concern, particularly in regions with lower vaccine coverage. The resurgence highlights the need for sustained efforts to maintain and improve vaccination rates.
Limitations and evidence strength
Several regions remain well below the Immunization Agenda 2030 (IA2030) target of 90% coverage. The study design limitations and the potential for underreporting in some regions may affect the accuracy of the results.

西得克萨斯州麻疹 暴发带给我的公共卫生领导力启示

What the West Texas Measles Outbreak Taught Me About Public Health Leadership
作者： Katherine Wells, Wells K., Katherine M Wells
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在探讨从西德克萨斯州 麻疹 暴发 在公共卫生领导和应对策略方面吸取的经验教训。这一背景凸显了麻疹在该地区的死灰复燃以及改善公共卫生管理的必要性。
设计与对象
该研究是对 2023 年至 2024 年期间进行的西德克萨斯州 麻疹 暴发 的回顾性分析。重点关注 麻疹 对公共卫生领导力的影响,并包含来自多个当地卫生部门的数据。
核心方法
这些方法包括审查 暴发 数据、分析公共卫生应对策略以及评估 暴发 期间领导决策的有效性。该研究依赖于对报告的定性分析和对公共卫生官员的采访。
主要结果
结果表明,暴发 强调了协调公共卫生领导、及时的 疫苗接种 活动和社区参与的重要性。它还揭示了沟通和资源分配方面的差距。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为呼吁在突发公共卫生事件中建立更强有力的领导框架。新颖之处在于强调领导力作为 暴发 管理的关键因素。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可以为制定更强大的公共卫生领导协议提供信息,并改善对未来 暴发 的准备。他们还强调在健康危机中需要更好的沟通策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性包括其对定性数据的依赖以及自我报告结果可能存在偏差。由于分析的描述性,证据是中等的。

What the West Texas Measles Outbreak Taught Me About Public Health Leadership

Authors: Katherine Wells, Wells K., Katherine M Wells
Original abstract (English)
Commentary What the West Texas Measles Outbreak Taught Me About Public Health Leadership - Katherine Wells Journal of Public Health Management & Practice 32(6):p 852-853, November/December 2026. | DOI: 10.1097/PHH.0000000000002404 Copyright © 2026 Wolters Kluwer Health, LLC. All rights reserved.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to examine the lessons learned from the West Texas measles outbreak regarding public health leadership and response strategies. The background highlighted the resurgence of measles in the region and the need for improved public health management.
Design and population
The study was a retrospective analysis of the West Texas measles outbreak conducted between 2023 and 2024. It focused on the outbreak's impact on public health leadership and included data from multiple local health departments.
Core methods
The methods involved reviewing outbreak data, analyzing public health response strategies, and evaluating the effectiveness of leadership decisions during the outbreak. The study relied on qualitative analysis of reports and interviews with public health officials.
Main results
The results indicated that the outbreak underscored the importance of coordinated public health leadership, timely vaccination campaigns, and community engagement. It also revealed gaps in communication and resource allocation.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as a call for stronger leadership frameworks in public health emergencies. The novelty lies in the emphasis on leadership as a critical factor in outbreak management.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the development of more robust public health leadership protocols and improve preparedness for future outbreaks. They also highlight the need for better communication strategies in health crises.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's limitations include its reliance on qualitative data and the potential for bias in self-reported outcomes. The evidence is moderate due to the descriptive nature of the analysis.

具有不完整 疫苗接种 和 1 个免疫力减弱的 麻疹 的 SVIR 模型分析:Sidoarjo-Indonesia 案例

SVIR Model Analysis of Measles with Incomplete Vaccination and 1 Waning Immunity: Sidoarjo-Indonesia Case
作者： Ainishafa Ainishafa, Abadi Abadi, Dian Savitri
摘要中文翻译麻疹仍然是印度尼西亚的重大公共卫生挑战,尤其是在疫苗接种覆盖率异质性和免疫力下降的地区。本研究开发并分析了一个针对麻疹传播的易感-接种-感染-康复(SVIR)分组模型,该模型纳入了免疫力下降的因素。基本再生数R0是通过下一代矩阵方法推导的,并且通过李雅普诺夫函数严格证明了无病平衡态和地方性平衡态的全局稳定性。模型参数是从2023年Sidoarjo市每周的监测数据中使用粒子群优化(PSO)、扩展卡尔曼滤波、最大似然估计和最小二乘法进行估计,并通过平均绝对百分比误差(MAPE)进行评估。PSO方法实现了最低的MAPE(11.17%),优于其他方法。估计的R0 = 0.668确认了无病状态。归一化敏感性分析确定疫苗效力是最关键的参数;疫苗效力下降至0 .75会使R0接近临界阈值,任何进一步的减少都会使系统进入地方性状态。这些发现强调了通过加强剂接种计划和强化疫苗接种策略保持高疫苗效力的必要性,以在区级维持麻疹的消除。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
使用下一代矩阵方法推导出基本繁殖数R0,并通过Lyapunov函数严格证明了无病平衡和地方病平衡的全局稳定性。
设计与对象
提供的证据没有明确说明研究设计、设置或研究人群。
核心方法
本研究开发并分析了印度尼西亚诗都阿佐市麻疹 传播 的易感-疫苗接种-感染-康复 (SVIR) 区室模型,其中考虑了免疫力的减弱。
主要结果
模型参数是根据西多阿佐市 2023 年每周的 监测 数据使用粒子群优化 (PSO)、扩展卡尔曼滤波器、最大似然估计和最小二乘法进行估计,并通过平均绝对百分比误差 (MAPE) 进行评估。PSO 实现了最低的 MAPE (11.17%),优于其他方法。
解释与创新
估计的 R0 = 0.668 确认无病状态。
科研与公卫意义
麻疹 在印度尼西亚仍然是一个重大的公共卫生挑战,特别是在 麻疹 覆盖范围不均匀且免疫力下降的地区。
局限与证据强度
Normalized sensitivity analysis identifies vaccineefficacy as the most influential parameter; a decline in efficacy to0 .75elevates R0 close tothe critical threshold, and any further reduction pushes the system into an endemic state.These findings underscore the necessity of maintaining high vaccine efficacy through boosterprograms and strengthened immunization strategies to sustain measles elimination at thedistrict level.

SVIR Model Analysis of Measles with Incomplete Vaccination and 1 Waning Immunity: Sidoarjo-Indonesia Case

Authors: Ainishafa Ainishafa, Abadi Abadi, Dian Savitri
Original abstract (English)
Measles remains a significant public health challenge in Indonesia, especially in regionswith heterogeneous vaccination coverage and waning immunity. This study develops andanalyzes a Susceptible–Vaccinated–Infected–Recovered (SVIR) compartmental model formeasles transmission in Sidoarjo City, Indonesia, incorporating waning immunity. The basicreproduction number R0 is derived using the next-generation matrix method, and the globalstability of both the disease-free and endemic equilibria is rigorously proven via Lyapunovfunctions. Model parameters are estimated from weekly 2023 surveillance data in Sidoarjo Cityusing Particle Swarm Optimization (PSO), Extended Kalman Filter, Maximum LikelihoodEstimation, and Least Squares, and evaluated via Mean Absolute Percentage Error (MAPE).PSO achieves the lowest MAPE (11.17%), outperforming the other methods. The estimatedR0 = 0.668confirms a disease-free regime. Normalized sensitivity analysis identifies vaccineefficacy as the most influential parameter; a decline in efficacy to0 .75elevates R0 close tothe critical threshold, and any further reduction pushes the system into an endemic state.These findings underscore the necessity of maintaining high vaccine efficacy through boosterprograms and strengthened immunization strategies to sustain measles elimination at thedistrict level.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The basicreproduction number R0 is derived using the next-generation matrix method, and the globalstability of both the disease-free and endemic equilibria is rigorously proven via Lyapunovfunctions.
Design and population
The supplied evidence does not clearly state the study design, setting, or study population.
Core methods
This study develops andanalyzes a Susceptible–Vaccinated–Infected–Recovered (SVIR) compartmental model formeasles transmission in Sidoarjo City, Indonesia, incorporating waning immunity.
Main results
Model parameters are estimated from weekly 2023 surveillance data in Sidoarjo Cityusing Particle Swarm Optimization (PSO), Extended Kalman Filter, Maximum LikelihoodEstimation, and Least Squares, and evaluated via Mean Absolute Percentage Error (MAPE).PSO achieves the lowest MAPE (11.17%), outperforming the other methods.
Interpretation and novelty
The estimatedR0 = 0.668confirms a disease-free regime.
Scientific and public-health significance
Measles remains a significant public health challenge in Indonesia, especially in regionswith heterogeneous vaccination coverage and waning immunity.
Limitations and evidence strength
Normalized sensitivity analysis identifies vaccineefficacy as the most influential parameter; a decline in efficacy to0 .75elevates R0 close tothe critical threshold, and any further reduction pushes the system into an endemic state.These findings underscore the necessity of maintaining high vaccine efficacy through boosterprograms and strengthened immunization strategies to sustain measles elimination at thedistrict level.

对 宿主 物种中麻疹病毒中枢神经系统持久性的比较分析揭示了与 亚急性硬化性全脑炎 的共同特征

Comparative analysis of central nervous system persistence of morbilliviruses across host species reveals shared features with subacute sclerosing panencephalitis
作者： Rik L. de Swart, Rory D. de Vries
摘要中文翻译结果,中枢神经系统中受感染的细胞不能产生病毒体,而是以缓慢但渐进的方式在细胞之间传播病毒核糖核蛋白,最终导致大脑广泛的 MeV 感染。结论 Daniel Roji 等人描述的与人类和动物麻疹病毒感染相关的神经系统并发症的比较。这些发现可能支持 SSPE 早期诊断或动物模型的开发,为测试新的干预策略提供机会。麻疹 相关免疫抑制导致机会性感染很常见,导致肺炎、腹泻和中耳炎等并发症[2]。这项研究说明了“One Health”方法对于理解 麻疹 神经系统并发症的复杂 麻疹 的重要性。M基因的失活可以通过引入过早终止密码子、起始密码子的损坏或导致产生生物失活蛋白质的偏向超突变来介导。因此,宿主 无法清除感染或阻止病毒传播到周围组织,导致病毒进入中枢神经系统的机会延长。摘要背景 亚急性硬化性全脑炎 (SSPE) 是一种罕见但总是致命的 麻疹 神经系统并发症,与中枢神经系统 (CNS) 的持续 麻疹病毒 (MeV) 感染相关。MeV 在已故 SSPE 患者的大脑中以超突变病毒 基因组 的形式被检测到,该病毒通常在基质 (M) 基因中包含过早终止密码子,并编码超融合融合 (F) 蛋白。然而,我们对 SSPE 发病机制 的理解受到动物模型缺乏以及 SSPE 临床静默潜伏期的限制。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在通过比较 宿主 物种中麻疹病毒的中枢神经系统持久性,了解 亚急性硬化性全脑炎 (SSPE) 的 发病机制,这是 麻疹 的一种罕见但致命的神经系统并发症。这一背景凸显了动物模型的缺乏和临床静默潜伏期是研究 SSPE 的限制因素。
设计与对象
该研究对持续感染的 宿主 大脑中检测到的动物麻疹病毒序列进行了比较分析,其中包括感染犬瘟热病毒 (CDV) 的山猫、感染福辛瘟热病毒 (PDV) 的两只海豹和感染鲸类麻疹病毒 (CeMV) 的海豚。
核心方法
作者分析了来自受感染 宿主 的动物麻疹病毒序列,重点关注病毒的遗传特征,特别是基质 (M) 基因和融合 (F) 蛋白,以确定独特的突变特征及其功能含义。
主要结果
来自受感染动物的麻疹病毒序列含有独特的突变特征,包括 CDV、CeMV 和一种编码非功能性 M 蛋白和超融合 F 蛋白的 PDV 序列,类似于 SSPE 患者中 麻疹病毒 (MeV) 的突变特征。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为证据,证明与慢性神经系统感染相关的人类和动物麻疹病毒具有共同的关键基因型和表型特征,为 SSPE 发病机制 提供了一种新颖的“One Health”视角。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能支持 SSPE 早期诊断或动物模型的开发,为测试这种致命神经并发症的新干预策略提供机会。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性在于其依赖于自然发生的动物感染,这可能无法完全代表人类 SSPE 发病机制。然而,证据是强有力的,因为它直接比较了不同物种的病毒序列和功能特征。

Comparative analysis of central nervous system persistence of morbilliviruses across host species reveals shared features with subacute sclerosing panencephalitis

Abstract Background Subacute sclerosing panencephalitis (SSPE) is a rare but always fatal neurological complication of measles, associated with persistent measles virus (MeV) infection of the central nervous system (CNS). MeV is detected in the brains of deceased SSPE patients as hypermutated viral genomes, which often contain premature stop codons in the matrix (M) gene and encode a hyperfusogenic fusion (F) protein. However, our understanding of the pathogenesis of SSPE is constrained by the lack of animal models combined with a clinically silent incubation period of s...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to understand the pathogenesis of subacute sclerosing panencephalitis (SSPE), a rare but fatal neurological complication of measles, by comparing the central nervous system persistence of morbilliviruses across host species. The background highlights the lack of animal models and the clinically silent incubation period as constraints to studying SSPE.
Design and population
The study involved a comparative analysis of animal morbillivirus sequences detected in the brains of persistently infected hosts, including a lynx infected with canine distemper virus (CDV), two seals infected with phocine distemper virus (PDV), and a dolphin infected with cetacean morbillivirus (CeMV).
Core methods
The authors analysed animal morbillivirus sequences from infected hosts, focusing on the genetic characteristics of the viruses, particularly the matrix (M) gene and fusion (F) protein, to identify unique mutational signatures and their functional implications.
Main results
The morbillivirus sequences from the infected animals contained unique mutational signatures, with CDV, CeMV, and one PDV sequence encoding nonfunctional M proteins and hyperfusogenic F proteins, similar to the mutational profile of measles virus (MeV) in SSPE patients.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted these findings as evidence that human and animal morbilliviruses associated with chronic neurological infections share key genotypic and phenotypic traits, offering a novel One Health perspective on SSPE pathogenesis.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings may support the development of early diagnostics or an animal model for SSPE, providing opportunities to test novel intervention strategies for this fatal neurological complication.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on naturally occurring animal infections, which may not fully represent human SSPE pathogenesis. However, the evidence is strong as it directly compares viral sequences and functional characteristics across species.

2025 Texas 麻疹 暴发 校准模型中的剂量加权 MMR 保护阈值和 暴发 控制

Dose-weighted MMR protection thresholds and outbreak control in a calibrated model of the 2025 Texas measles outbreak
作者： Shakirat Adetomiwa, Bismark Oduro, Lanre Akinyemi
摘要中文翻译这些发现支持优先考虑消除零剂量和总体 疫苗接种 差距,同时保持及时完成两剂 MMR。数据可用性 当前研究期间没有生成或分析数据集。https://doi.org/10.15585/mmwr.mm7045a1 - Guerra FM,Bolotin S,Lim G,Heffernan J,Deeks SL,Li Y,Crowcroft NS (2017) 麻疹 的基本复制数 \((R_{0})\):系统评价。2025 年 7 月 26 日检索自 https://www.who.int/emergcies/disease-疫苗-news/item/2025-DON561 - Phadke VK、Bednarczyk RA、Salmon DA、Omer SB (2016) 疫苗 拒绝与 疫苗 可预防之间的关联美国的疾病:麻疹 和百日咳的回顾。摘要 2025 年德克萨斯州 麻疹 暴发 强调了 麻疹-腮腺炎-风疹 (MMR) 疫苗接种 中局部间隙在维持 传播 中的作用。在本文中,我们开发了一种确定性剂量结构的 麻疹 模型,用于区分未接种疫苗、接种一剂疫苗和接种两剂疫苗的易感个体,以及残留敏感性、临床并发症和随时间变化的 传播。该模型通过非线性最小二乘法校准到报告的德克萨斯州 发病率 的 24 周,并重现了每周 暴发 模式,均方根误差为 4.7594,确定系数为 0.9692。在 MMR 总覆盖率 82%、两剂完成率 80% 的基线情景下,平均控制繁殖数为 \(R_{c,m}=1.3537\),表明 暴发 控制的人群保护不足。在拟合条件下,有效的 疫苗 诱导的群体保护必须超过 84.40% 才能将 \(R_{c,m}\) 降低到 1 以下,对应于 80% 的两剂完成时的临界总 MMR 覆盖率为 87.74%。仅增加两剂完成就会产生...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
2025 年德克萨斯州 麻疹 暴发 强调了 麻疹-腮腺炎-风疹 (MMR) 疫苗接种 中局部缺口在维持 传播 中的作用。该研究旨在确定 疫苗接种 控制所需的 疫苗接种 覆盖范围和剂量完成阈值。
设计与对象
开发了确定性剂量结构的 麻疹 模型,用于区分未接种疫苗、接种一剂疫苗和接种两剂疫苗的易感个体,以及残留敏感性、临床并发症和随时间变化的 传播。该模型根据 24 周报告的德克萨斯州 发病率 数据进行校准。该研究重点关注 2025 年德克萨斯州的 麻疹 暴发。
核心方法
该模型使用非线性最小二乘法对 24 周报告的德克萨斯州 发病率 数据进行校准,再现每周 暴发 模式,均方根误差为 4.7594,确定系数为 0.9692。该框架将 疫苗接种 覆盖范围、剂量完成、剂量特异性 疫苗 有效性和 传播 条件与 暴发 控制阈值联系起来。
主要结果
在 MMR 总覆盖率 82% 和两剂完成 80% 的基线情景下,平均控制繁殖数为 $R_{c,m}=1.3537$,表明 暴发 控制的人群保护不足。在检查的所有完成场景中,将 MMR 总覆盖率提高到 90% 将 $R_{c,m}$ 降低到 1 以下。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,实现更高的 MMR 总覆盖率比单独增加两剂接种完成率更能有效地减少繁殖数量。该研究为理解与 疫苗接种 策略相关的 暴发 控制阈值提供了一个新颖的分析框架。
科研与公卫意义
结果通过强调缩小 疫苗接种 差距和确保及时完成两剂 MMR 以有效控制 麻疹 暴发 的重要性,为公共卫生策略提供信息。他们还强调需要对 疫苗接种 覆盖率低的人群进行有针对性的干预措施。
局限与证据强度
该研究依赖于校准模型,不包括来自现实世界干预措施的经验数据。证据强度中等,因为基于模型的研究结果受到假设和参数不确定性的影响。

Dose-weighted MMR protection thresholds and outbreak control in a calibrated model of the 2025 Texas measles outbreak

Authors: Shakirat Adetomiwa, Bismark Oduro, Lanre Akinyemi
Original abstract (English)
These findings support prioritizing closure of zero-dose and overall vaccination gaps while maintaining timely two-dose MMR completion. Retrieved July 26, 2025, from https://www.who.int/emergencies/disease-outbreak-news/item/2025-DON561 - Phadke VK, Bednarczyk RA, Salmon DA, Omer SB (2016) Association between vaccine refusal and vaccine-preventable diseases in the United States: A review of measles and pertussis. Abstract The 2025 Texas measles outbreak highlighted the role of localized gaps in measles–mumps–rubella (MMR) vaccination in sustaining transmission. In this paper, we developed a deterministic dose-structured measles model distinguishing unvaccinated, one-dose vaccinated, and two-dose vaccinated susceptible individuals, with residual susceptibility, clinical complications, and time-varying transmission. The model was calibrated by nonlinear least squares to 24 weeks of reported Texas incidence and reproduced the weekly outbreak pattern with a root mean square error of 4.7594 and a coefficient of determination of 0.9692. Under the baseline scenario of 82% total MMR coverage and 80% two-dose completion, the mean controlled reproduction number was \(R_{c,m}=1.3537\), indicating insufficient population protection for outbreak control. Under the fitted conditions, effective vaccine-induced population protection had to exceed 84.40% to reduce \(R_{c,m}\) below one, corresponding to a critical total MMR coverage of 87.74% at 80% two-dose completion. View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The 2025 Texas measles outbreak highlighted the role of localized gaps in measles–mumps–rubella (MMR) vaccination in sustaining transmission. The study aimed to determine the vaccination coverage and dose completion thresholds necessary for outbreak control.
Design and population
A deterministic dose-structured measles model was developed to distinguish unvaccinated, one-dose vaccinated, and two-dose vaccinated susceptible individuals, with residual susceptibility, clinical complications, and time-varying transmission. The model was calibrated to 24 weeks of reported Texas incidence data. The study focused on measles outbreaks in Texas during 2025.
Core methods
The model was calibrated using nonlinear least squares to 24 weeks of reported Texas incidence data, reproducing the weekly outbreak pattern with a root mean square error of 4.7594 and a coefficient of determination of 0.9692. The framework links vaccination coverage, dose completion, dose-specific vaccine effectiveness, and transmission conditions to outbreak-control thresholds.
Main results
Under the baseline scenario of 82% total MMR coverage and 80% two-dose completion, the mean controlled reproduction number was $R_{c,m}=1.3537$, indicating insufficient population protection for outbreak control. Increasing total MMR coverage to 90% reduced $R_{c,m}$ below one in all completion scenarios examined.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings demonstrate that achieving higher total MMR coverage is more effective in reducing the reproduction number than increasing two-dose completion alone. The study provides a novel analytical framework for understanding outbreak-control thresholds in relation to vaccination strategies.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results inform public health strategies by emphasizing the importance of closing vaccination gaps and ensuring timely two-dose MMR completion to effectively control measles outbreaks. They also highlight the need for targeted interventions in populations with low vaccination coverage.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study relies on a calibrated model and does not include empirical data from real-world interventions. The evidence strength is moderate, as the model-based findings are subject to assumptions and parameter uncertainties.

成人 麻疹 的免疫病理遗传学特征:麻疹 状态与 麻疹 特异性 IgM 水平和细胞因子谱(IL-1β、IL-10)的关联

Immunopathogenetic Features of Measles in Adults: Association of Vaccination Status with Measles-Specific IgM Levels and Cytokine Profile (IL-1β, IL-10)
作者： Olha Matviiuk, Oleksandra Pryshliak, Oleksandr BOICHUK, Ihor Hryzhak, Zoriana Tylishchak, Taras Kobryn, Ruslan Miziuk, Petro Gerych
摘要中文翻译Olha Matviiuk,1,2 Oleksandra Pryshliak,1,2 Oleksandr Boichuk,1,2 Ihor Hryzhak,1,2 Zoriana Tylishchak,1,2 Taras Kobryn,1,2 Ruslan Miziuk,1,2 Petro Gerych3,41伊万诺-弗兰科夫斯克国立医科大学传染病系和 流行病学,乌克兰伊万诺-弗兰科夫斯克;2 市级非商业企业“传染病中心”,伊万诺-弗兰科夫斯克,乌克兰;3 伊万诺-弗兰科夫斯克国立医科大学内科第 1 系,以 E.M. Neiko 院士命名的临床免疫学和变态反应学,乌克兰伊万诺-弗兰科夫斯克;4 乌克兰伊万诺-弗兰科夫斯克国立医科大学跨部门科学实验室通讯作者:乌克兰伊万诺-弗兰科夫斯克国立医科大学传染病系 Olha Matviiuk 和 流行病学,电子邮件 yaciv.olya@gmail.com 背景:先前 麻疹 的影响疫苗接种 在成人急性 麻疹 期间对体液和细胞因子 免疫应答 的影响仍不充分。本研究根据记录的 麻疹 状态评估了 麻疹 特异性 IgM 抗体水平、白细胞介素 (IL)-1β 和 IL-10 的早期动态及其与血液学参数的关联。方法:这项单中心观察性研究包括 80 名具有临床相容性 麻疹 且 麻疹 特异性 IgM 结果呈阳性的成年人,他们在住院 3-5 天后住院中度或重度疾病发作。患者被分类为有记录的既往 麻疹 疫苗接种 (n = 40) 或没有记录的既往 麻疹 疫苗接种 证据 (n = 40)。在皮疹发作后第 7 天通过 ELISA 测量 麻疹 特异性 IgM 抗体。入院时和住院第 3 天评估血清 IL-1β、IL-10、白细胞总数和相对淋巴细胞百分比。采用非参数统计方法进行组间比较和相关分析。进行额外的回归分析以评估调整疾病严重程度后 IL-10 的组间差异。结果:记录有 麻疹 的患者具有显着较高的 麻疹 特异性 IgM 水平。从入院到第 3 天,两组的 IL-1β 均显着下降,IL-10 显着升高,但组间 IL-1β 没有差异。在未经调整的分析中,在两个时间点没有记录 疫苗接种 的患者中 IL-10 较高。调整疾病严重程度后,入院时 IL-10 差异不再显着,而第 3 天的差异仍然显着。在两组中均观察到 IL-1β 和 IL-10 之间存在强正相关性,而在没有记录 麻疹 的患者中,IgM 与相对淋巴细胞百分比呈正相关。结论:先前记录的 麻疹 疫苗接种 与较高的 麻疹 特异性 IgM 水平相关,而在调整疾病严重程度后,第 3 天 IL-10 的组间差异仍然存在。各组之间的 IL-1β 动态相似。这些发现表明,有记录的既往 麻疹 疫苗接种 的成年人和没有记录的 麻疹 证据的成年人之间的免疫反应模式存在差异,同时支持对这些关联的谨慎解释。关键词:麻疹、细胞因子、疫苗接种、免疫发病机制、免疫球蛋白、危险因素、疾病结果
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查成人急性 麻疹 期间先前的 麻疹 疫苗接种 对体液和细胞因子 免疫应答 的影响,因为这一点尚未充分表征。
设计与对象
这项单中心观察性研究纳入了 80 名临床上符合 麻疹 且 麻疹 特异性 IgM 结果呈阳性的成年人,他们在中度或重度疾病发作后 3-5 天住院。患者被分类为有记录的既往 麻疹 疫苗接种 (n = 40) 或没有记录的既往 麻疹 疫苗接种 证据 (n = 40)。
核心方法
在皮疹发作后第 7 天通过 ELISA 测量 麻疹 特异性 IgM 抗体。入院时和住院第 3 天评估血清 IL-1β、IL-10、白细胞总数和相对淋巴细胞百分比。采用非参数统计方法进行组间比较和相关分析。
主要结果
记录有 疫苗接种 的患者的 麻疹 特异性 IgM 水平显着较高。从入院到第 3 天,两组的 IL-1β 均显着下降,IL-10 显着升高,但组间 IL-1β 没有差异。在两组中均观察到 IL-1β 和 IL-10 之间存在强正相关性,而在没有 疫苗接种 记录的患者中,IgM 与相对淋巴细胞百分比呈正相关。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,有记录在先的 麻疹 疫苗接种 的成年人和没有记录在案的 疫苗接种 证据的成年人之间的免疫反应模式存在差异,强调需要谨慎解释这些关联。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果可能有助于理解 麻疹 的免疫反应模式,可能有助于为具有不同 麻疹 病史的成年人制定有针对性的诊断或治疗策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到单中心设计和观察性质的限制,这可能会影响普遍性。由于缺乏随机性和潜在的混杂因素,证据强度中等。

Immunopathogenetic Features of Measles in Adults: Association of Vaccination Status with Measles-Specific IgM Levels and Cytokine Profile (IL-1β, IL-10)

Authors: Olha Matviiuk, Oleksandra Pryshliak, Oleksandr BOICHUK, Ihor Hryzhak, Zoriana Tylishchak, Taras Kobryn, Ruslan Miziuk, Petro Gerych
Original abstract (English)
Olha Matviiuk,1,2 Oleksandra Pryshliak,1,2 Oleksandr Boichuk,1,2 Ihor Hryzhak,1,2 Zoriana Tylishchak,1,2 Taras Kobryn,1,2 Ruslan Miziuk,1,2 Petro Gerych3,41Department of Infectious Diseases and Epidemiology, Ivano-Frankivsk National Medical University, Ivano-Frankivsk, Ukraine; 2Municipal Non-Commercial Enterprise "Center for Infectious Diseases”, Ivano-Frankivsk, Ukraine; 3Department of Internal Medicine No. 1, Clinical Immunology and Allergology Named After Academician E.M. Neiko, Ivano-Frankivsk National Medical University, Ivano-Frankivsk, Ukraine; 4Interdepartmental Scientific Laboratory, Ivano-Frankivsk National Medical University, Ivano-Frankivsk, UkraineCorrespondence: Olha Matviiuk, Department of Infectious Diseases and Epidemiology, Ivano-Frankivsk National Medical University, Ivano-Frankivsk, Ukraine, Email yaciv.olya@gmail.comBackground: The influence of prior measles vaccination on humoral and cytokine immune responses during acute measles in adults remains insufficiently characterized. This study evaluated measles-specific IgM antibody levels, the early dynamics of interleukin (IL)-1β and IL-10, and their associations with hematological parameters according to documented vaccination status.Methods: This single-center observational study included 80 adults with clinically compatible measles and positive measles-specific IgM results who were hospitalized 3– 5 days after disease onset with moderate or severe illness. Patients were classified as having documented prior measles vaccination (n = 40) or no documented evidence of prior measles vaccination (n = 40). Measles-specific IgM antibodies were measured by ELISA on day 7 after rash onset. Serum IL-1β, IL-10, total leukocyte count, and relative lymphocyte percentage were assessed at admission and on day 3 of hospitalization. Non-parametric statistical methods were applied for group comparisons and correlation analyses. An additional regression analysis was performed to assess between-group differences in IL-10 after adjustment for disease severity.Results: Patients with documented vaccination had significantly higher measles-specific IgM levels. IL-1β decreased and IL-10 increased significantly from admission to day 3 in both groups, with no between-group differences in IL-1β. In unadjusted analyses, IL-10 was higher in patients without documented vaccination at both time points. After adjustment for disease severity, the IL-10 difference at admission was no longer significant, whereas the difference on day 3 remained significant. Strong positive correlations between IL-1β and IL-10 were observed in both groups, while IgM correlated positively with relative lymphocyte percentage in patients without documented vaccination.Conclusion: Documented prior measles vaccination was associated with higher measles-specific IgM levels, while the between-group difference in IL-10 on day 3 persisted after adjustment for disease severity. IL-1β dynamics were similar between the groups. These findings demonstrate differences in immune-response patterns between adults with documented prior measles vaccination and those without documented evidence of vaccination, while supporting cautious interpretation of these associations.Keywords: measles, cytokines, vaccination, immunopathogenesis, immunoglobulin, risk factor, disease outcome
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the influence of prior measles vaccination on humoral and cytokine immune responses during acute measles in adults, as this remains insufficiently characterized.
Design and population
This single-center observational study included 80 adults with clinically compatible measles and positive measles-specific IgM results who were hospitalized 3–5 days after disease onset with moderate or severe illness. Patients were classified as having documented prior measles vaccination (n = 40) or no documented evidence of prior measles vaccination (n = 40).
Core methods
Measles-specific IgM antibodies were measured by ELISA on day 7 after rash onset. Serum IL-1β, IL-10, total leukocyte count, and relative lymphocyte percentage were assessed at admission and on day 3 of hospitalization. Non-parametric statistical methods were applied for group comparisons and correlation analyses.
Main results
Patients with documented vaccination had significantly higher measles-specific IgM levels. IL-1β decreased and IL-10 increased significantly from admission to day 3 in both groups, with no between-group differences in IL-1β. Strong positive correlations between IL-1β and IL-10 were observed in both groups, while IgM correlated positively with relative lymphocyte percentage in patients without documented vaccination.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings demonstrate differences in immune-response patterns between adults with documented prior measles vaccination and those without documented evidence of vaccination, highlighting the need for cautious interpretation of these associations.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results may inform the understanding of immune-response patterns in measles, potentially aiding in the development of targeted diagnostic or therapeutic strategies for adults with varying vaccination histories.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its single-center design and observational nature, which may affect generalisability. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the lack of randomization and potential confounding factors.

Journal: Genetics and molecular biology  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-10-19  |  DOI: 10.1590/1678-4685-gmb-2025-0075  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 49, 3, e20250075

HVSnp:手动整理的与病毒感染相关的人类 SNPS 数据库。

HVSnp: A manually curated database of human SNPS associated with viral infections.
作者： Ana Carolina Laureano Paganin, Caio César de Melo Freire
摘要中文翻译摘要 我们开发了“HVSnp:人类病毒 SNP 相关数据库,一个精选数据库”。HVSnp 专门研究与病毒性疾病相关的人类 SNP,并从公共 SNP 数据库和科学文章中手动挖掘和整理信息。由于管理方便且 Looker Studio 在线可用,我们使用了 MySQL(版本 8.0)。在输出信息中,我们有SNP识别、参考、替代和风险等位基因、染色体、基因和基因功能、摘要等。HVSnp 总共包含 18 种病毒性疾病和 2020 条记录:腺病毒 (17 个 SNP)、基孔肯雅热 (7)、COVID-19 (314)、巨细胞病毒 (54)、登革热 (62)、Epstein-Barr (54)、甲型肝炎 (3)、乙型肝炎 (380)、丙型肝炎 (166)、疱疹 (8)、人类免疫缺陷病毒(479)、乳头瘤病毒 (87)、流感 (74)、麻疹 (68)、呼吸道合胞病毒 (50)、SARS-CoV (80)、西尼罗河病毒 (68) 和寨卡病毒 (49)。由于一个SNP可以与不止一种病毒相关,因此独特SNP的数量为1691个和651个基因。HVSnp 在文章中获得了用于总结阐述的补充信息,其中包含文献中描述的有关 SNP 和病毒之间可能关系的最重要结果。该数据库可通过以下网址获取:https://lookerstudio.google.com/s/pGUC7m538gQ。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在开发一个与病毒感染相关的人类单核苷酸多态性(SNP)的精选数据库,满足对了解病毒性疾病遗传基础的综合资源的需求。
设计与对象
该研究涉及创建 HVSnp,该数据库包含与 18 种病毒性疾病相关的 2020 年 SNP 记录,包括 麻疹,使用公共 SNP 数据库和科学文章的手动管理。
核心方法
HVSnp 是使用 MySQL(版本 8.0)进行数据库管理和 Looker Studio 进行在线可用性开发的。
主要结果
HVSnp 包括 SNP 鉴定、参考、替代和风险等位基因、染色体、基因和基因功能,共有 1691 个独特的 SNP 和 651 个基因。
解释与创新
该数据库提供了文章的补充信息,详细阐述了有关 SNP 与病毒之间关系的最重要结果,强调了病毒性疾病的潜在遗传基础。
科研与公卫意义
HVSnp 可以通过提供全面的资源来了解与这些感染相关的遗传因素,从而告知 监测、诊断 和 治疗 病毒性疾病。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性包括手动管理过程以及选择 SNP 和病毒时可能存在的偏差,这可能会影响数据库的全面性和准确性。

HVSnp: A manually curated database of human SNPS associated with viral infections.

Authors: Ana Carolina Laureano Paganin, Caio César de Melo Freire
Original abstract (English)
Abstract We developed the “HVSnp: Human Viruses SNP-related database, a curated database”. HVSnp is exclusive to the human SNPs studied and related to viral diseases, with manually mined and curated information from public SNP databases and scientific articles. We used MySQL (version 8.0) due to the easy management and the Looker Studio online availability. Among the output information, we have SNP identification, reference, alternative, and risk alleles, chromosome, gene, and gene function, summary, among others. In total, HVSnp contains eighteen viral diseases and 2020 records: Adenovirus (17 SNPs), Chikungunya (7), COVID-19 (314), Cytomegalovirus (54), Dengue (62), Epstein-Barr (54), Hepatitis A (3), Hepatitis B (380), Hepatitis C (166), Herpes (8), Human immunodeficiency virus (479), Papillomavirus (87), Influenza (74), Measles (68), Respiratory syncytial virus (50), Sars-CoV (80), West Nile (68), and Zika (49). As one SNP can be associated with more than one virus, the number of unique SNPs is 1691 and 651 genes. HVSnp has supplementary information obtained in articles for the summary elaboration, which contains the most significant results described in the literature about a possible relationship between SNPs and viruses. The database is available at URL: https://lookerstudio.google.com/s/pGUC7m538gQ.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to develop a curated database of human SNPs associated with viral infections, addressing the need for a comprehensive resource to understand the genetic basis of viral diseases.
Design and population
The study involved the creation of HVSnp, a database containing 2020 records of SNPs related to 18 viral diseases, including measles, using manual curation from public SNP databases and scientific articles.
Core methods
HVSnp was developed using MySQL (version 8.0) for database management and Looker Studio for online availability.
Main results
HVSnp includes SNP identification, reference, alternative, and risk alleles, chromosome, gene, and gene function, with a total of 1691 unique SNPs and 651 genes.
Interpretation and novelty
The database provides supplementary information from articles to elaborate on the most significant results about the relationship between SNPs and viruses, highlighting the potential genetic basis of viral diseases.
Scientific and public-health significance
HVSnp can inform surveillance, diagnosis, and treatment of viral diseases by providing a comprehensive resource for understanding the genetic factors associated with these infections.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's limitations include the manual curation process and the potential for bias in the selection of SNPs and viruses, which may affect the comprehensiveness and accuracy of the database.

MethyLiverDx-Sigma(一种用于肝细胞癌检测的血浆 cfDNA 甲基化检测)的分析验证。

Analytical Validation of MethyLiverDx-Sigma, a Plasma cfDNA Methylation Assay for Hepatocellular Carcinoma Detection.
作者： Si-Cho Kim, Hyunwoo Choi, Jiin Chung, Seung-Taek Lee, Young-Joon Kim
摘要中文翻译肝细胞癌 监测 依赖超声检查和甲胎蛋白,两者对早期疾病的敏感性有限;循环游离 DNA (cfDNA) 的异常甲基化提供了一种有前途的基于血液的方法。这里报告的是 MethyLiverDx-Sigma (MLDx-s) 的分析验证,该方法通过具有高分辨率熔解 (HRM) 的甲基化特异性 PCR 检测重亚硫酸盐转化的血浆 cfDNA 中的甲基化 RNF135 和 DNMT3A,并以 GAPDH 作为内源对照。根据 CLSI EP05-A3、EP07、EP12-A2 和 EP37 评估性能。通过一个站点内以及两个站点和两个操作员之间的方差分量分析来评估定量周期 (Cq) 和熔解峰温度 (Tm) 的精度。由于精密研究使用大量亚硫酸氢盐转化的参考 DNA,因此报告的变异系数 (CV) 主要表征 PCR-HRM 检测模块;临床血浆样本的端到端变异性可能更高。通过反应物质的剂量反应测试,根据再现性限制,对低阳性和人为阴性样品中的干扰和交叉反应性进行了评估。两个水平的检测均为 100%,并且每次运行均符合对照有效性标准。Cq 重复性 CV 范围为 0.53% 至 1.68%,总再现性 CV 范围为 1.10% 至 2.37%;所有 Tm CV 均≤0.20%。在低阳性样本中评估的所有 18 种干扰物和 16 种微生物交叉反应物均符合预定的验收标准。在阴性样本中,血红蛋白、SARS-CoV-2 和 麻疹病毒 产生浓度依赖性非特异性扩增,剂量反应测试确定了它们各自的无作用浓度。这些发现表征了 PCR-HRM 检测模块的分析精度和多位点重现性以及 MLDx-s 的分析特异性,补充了其先前报道的基于血浆的 HCC 检测的临床性能。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在验证一种利用循环游离 DNA (cfDNA) 异常甲基化检测肝细胞癌 (HCC) 的血液检测方法,解决超声检查和甲胎蛋白等现有方法不足以进行早期疾病检测的局限性。
设计与对象
该研究涉及 MethyLiverDx-Sigma (MLDx-s) 的分析验证,该方法使用具有高分辨率熔解 (HRM) 的甲基化特异性 PCR 来检测血浆 cfDNA 中的甲基化 RNF135 和 DNMT3A。验证遵循 CLSI 指南,包括跨多个站点和操作员的精度和可重复性评估。
核心方法
MLDx-s 的分析验证是使用 CLSI EP05-A3、EP07、EP12-A2 和 EP37 指南进行的。通过一个站点内以及两个站点和两个操作员之间的方差分量分析来评估定量周期 (Cq) 和熔解峰温度 (Tm) 的精度。
主要结果
Cq 重复性变异系数 (CV) 范围为 0.53% 至 1.68%,总再现性 CV 范围为 1.10% 至 2.37%。所有 Tm CV 均≤0.20%。此外,所有 18 种干扰物和 16 种微生物交叉反应物均符合预定的验收标准。
解释与创新
这些发现确立了 PCR-HRM 检测模块的分析精度和多位点重现性,以及 MLDx-s 的分析特异性,这补充了其先前报道的基于血浆的 HCC 检测的临床性能。
科研与公卫意义
结果为 MLDx-s 在 HCC 监测 中的使用奠定了基础,为早期检测有限的现有方法(例如超声检查和甲胎蛋白)提供了更灵敏和特异的替代方法。
局限与证据强度
该研究的重点是使用参考 DNA 进行分析验证,并未评估临床血浆样本的端到端变异性,这可能会带来额外的不确定性。由于研究结果仅具有抽象性,因此证据强度中等。

Analytical Validation of MethyLiverDx-Sigma, a Plasma cfDNA Methylation Assay for Hepatocellular Carcinoma Detection.

Authors: Si-Cho Kim, Hyunwoo Choi, Jiin Chung, Seung-Taek Lee, Young-Joon Kim
Original abstract (English)
Hepatocellular carcinoma surveillance relies on ultrasonography and alpha-fetoprotein, both limited in sensitivity for early-stage disease; aberrant methylation of circulating cell-free DNA (cfDNA) offers a promising blood-based approach. Reported here is the analytical validation of MethyLiverDx-Sigma (MLDx-s), an assay that detects methylated RNF135 and DNMT3A in bisulfite-converted plasma cfDNA by methylation-specific PCR with high-resolution melting (HRM), with GAPDH as an endogenous control. Performance was evaluated following CLSI EP05-A3, EP07, EP12-A2, and EP37. Precision of quantification cycle (Cq) and melting peak temperature (Tm) was assessed by variance-component analysis within one site and across two sites and two operators. Because precision studies used bulk bisulfite-converted reference DNA, the reported coefficients of variation (CVs) primarily characterize the PCR-HRM detection module; end-to-end variability in clinical plasma specimens may be higher. Interference and cross-reactivity were evaluated in low-positive and contrived-negative samples against reproducibility-derived limits, with dose-response testing of reactive substances. Detection was 100% at both levels, and every run met control-validity criteria. Cq repeatability CVs ranged from 0.53% to 1.68%, and total reproducibility CVs ranged from 1.10% to 2.37%; all Tm CVs were ≤0.20%. All 18 interferents and 16 microbial cross-reactants evaluated in low-positive samples met the predefined acceptance criteria. In negative samples, hemoglobin, SARS-CoV-2, and measles virus produced concentration-dependent non-specific amplification, and dose-response testing established their respective no-effect concentrations. These findings characterize the analytical precision and multisite reproducibility of the PCR-HRM detection module and the analytical specificity of MLDx-s, complementing its previously reported clinical performance for plasma-based HCC detection.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to validate a blood-based assay for detecting hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) using aberrant methylation of circulating cell-free DNA (cfDNA), addressing the limitations of current methods like ultrasonography and alpha-fetoprotein which are insufficient for early-stage disease detection.
Design and population
The study involved analytical validation of MethyLiverDx-Sigma (MLDx-s), an assay that detects methylated RNF135 and DNMT3A in plasma cfDNA using methylation-specific PCR with high-resolution melting (HRM). The validation followed CLSI guidelines and included precision and reproducibility assessments across multiple sites and operators.
Core methods
The analytical validation of MLDx-s was conducted using CLSI EP05-A3, EP07, EP12-A2, and EP37 guidelines. Precision of quantification cycle (Cq) and melting peak temperature (Tm) was evaluated through variance-component analysis within one site and across two sites and two operators.
Main results
Cq repeatability coefficients of variation (CVs) ranged from 0.53% to 1.68%, and total reproducibility CVs ranged from 1.10% to 2.37%. All Tm CVs were ≤0.20%. Additionally, all 18 interferents and 16 microbial cross-reactants met predefined acceptance criteria.
Interpretation and novelty
These findings establish the analytical precision and multisite reproducibility of the PCR-HRM detection module, as well as the analytical specificity of MLDx-s, which complements its previously reported clinical performance for plasma-based HCC detection.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results provide a foundation for the use of MLDx-s in HCC surveillance, offering a more sensitive and specific alternative to current methods such as ultrasonography and alpha-fetoprotein, which are limited in early detection.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study focused on analytical validation using reference DNA and did not evaluate end-to-end variability in clinical plasma specimens, which may introduce additional uncertainty. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the abstract-only nature of the findings.

冲突限制下的地方病控制模型:地区政府帮助苏丹因战争而孤立的人群的能力

Modeling Endemic Disease Control Under Conflict Constraints: Regional Government Capacity to Reach Populations Isolated by Warfare in Sudan
作者： Emile Franc Doungmo Goufo
摘要中文翻译武装冲突严重扰乱了公共卫生系统,但人们对暴力与地方病 暴发 之间的联系机制仍知之甚少。本研究探讨了 2000 年至 2024 年间暴力冲突如何影响苏丹受冲突影响地区的疾病治理和卫生服务提供。该分析使用空间面板数据集,结合武装冲突地点和事件数据项目 (ACLED) 的地理参考冲突事件、流行病学 监测 记录、环境指标和地方治理措施,评估冲突强度、治理能力和地方病 发病率 之间的关系。结果表明,暴力程度较高的地区的疟疾、霍乱和 麻疹 的 发病率 显着较高。在卫生基础设施遭到破坏和行政能力削弱的情况下,这些影响尤其明显。地方治理较强的地区表现出更强的复原力,尽管冲突仍在持续,但仍然保持部分 疫苗接种 覆盖和疾病 监测。空间模型进一步揭示了显着的 跨物种溢出 效应,暴发 沿着位移走廊和边界区域传播。研究结果强调了治理能力在调解武装冲突期间健康结果方面的核心作用,并强调了冲突敏感型卫生干预措施的重要性。加强分散的卫生治理、保护卫生基础设施和改善跨区域疾病 监测 对于减轻脆弱和受冲突影响地区的地方病风险至关重要。

问题与背景
武装冲突严重扰乱了公共卫生系统,但人们对暴力与地方病 暴发 之间的联系机制仍知之甚少。
设计与对象
本研究探讨了 2000 年至 2024 年间暴力冲突如何影响苏丹受冲突影响地区的疾病治理和卫生服务提供。
核心方法
该分析使用空间面板数据集,结合了武装冲突位置和事件数据项目 (ACLED) 中的地理参考冲突事件、流行病学 监测 记录、环境指标和地方治理措施,评估了冲突强度、治理能力和地方病 发病率 之间的关系。
主要结果
结果表明,暴力程度较高的地区的疟疾、霍乱和 麻疹 的 发病率 显着较高。
解释与创新
在卫生基础设施遭到破坏和行政能力削弱的情况下,这些影响尤其明显。
科研与公卫意义
地方治理较强的地区表现出更强的复原力,尽管冲突仍在持续,但仍然保持部分 疫苗接种 覆盖和疾病 监测。
局限与证据强度
空间模型进一步揭示了显着的 跨物种溢出 效应,暴发 沿着位移走廊和边界区域传播。

Modeling Endemic Disease Control Under Conflict Constraints: Regional Government Capacity to Reach Populations Isolated by Warfare in Sudan

Authors: Emile Franc Doungmo Goufo
Original abstract (English)
Armed conflict significantly disrupts public health systems, yet the mechanisms linking violence to endemic disease outbreaks remain insufficiently understood. This study examines how violent conflict affects disease governance and health service delivery across Sudan’s conflict-affected regions between 2000 and 2024. Using a spatial panel dataset that combines geo-referenced conflict events from the Armed Conflict Location and Event Data Project (ACLED), epidemiological surveillance records, environmental indicators, and subnational governance measures, the analysis evaluates the relationship between conflict intensity, governance capacity, and endemic disease incidence. The results indicate that areas experiencing higher levels of violence exhibit significantly greater incidence of malaria, cholera, and measles. These effects are particularly pronounced where health infrastructure has been damaged and administrative capacity has weakened. Regions with stronger local governance demonstrate greater resilience, maintaining partial vaccination coverage and disease surveillance despite ongoing conflict. Spatial models further reveal significant spillover effects, with outbreaks spreading along displacement corridors and border regions. The findings highlight the central role of governance capacity in mediating health outcomes during armed conflict and underscore the importance of conflict-sensitive health interventions. Strengthening decentralized health governance, protecting health infrastructure, and improving cross-regional disease surveillance are essential for mitigating endemic disease risks in fragile and conflict-affected settings.

Spatial models further reveal significant spillover effects, with outbreaks spreading along displacement corridors and border regions.

群体免疫吸引子:1980-2025 年 195 个国家 疫苗接种 的选择、漂移和稳态供应不足

The herd immunity attractor: selection, drift, and the steady-state undersupply of vaccination in 195 countries, 1980–2025
作者： Aras Yolusever
摘要中文翻译简介 疫苗接种 是卫生经济学中典型的正外部性:因为每个 疫苗接种 都保护其他人,所以预计去中心化选择将停滞在群体免疫阈值以下。然而,关于支配全国范围的运动定律的证据却很少。这项研究直接估计了它。方法我将异质家庭人口的接种或不接种决策建模为预期效用问题,其净收益随着疫苗接种份额的下降而下降,通过明确的修订方案将其汇总为选择突变(复制子-变异子)方程,其中收益响应切换与卫生系统接触和损耗风险共存,并根据世界卫生组织/联合国儿童基金会(WHO/UNICEF)估计所得方程1980-2025 年 195 个国家的国家免疫覆盖率估计(WUENIC,2025 年修订版),采用联合国 队列研究 分母和世界银行收入和卫生系统能力控制。精确的识别结果表明,线性周转系数等于完全覆盖和零覆盖时运动定律之间的差,因此它是根据与支付参数无关的边界观察来识别的,而支付斜率仅在覆盖穿过单纯形的地方被识别。结果首先,在覆盖范围内部,动态以强线性恢复力为主,这意味着相关池的年周转率为15%–25%;那里的收益斜率在统计上很弱,并且内部数据无法区分制度流动和对收益不敏感的修正,因此估计的风险从上面限制了制度成分。其次,搭便车特征出现在它被识别的地方:在跨越整个覆盖范围的 Hib 推出中急剧变化,而在第一剂 DTP 裕度上较弱。第三,每个国家的运动规律都有一个稳定的内部休息点;第一剂 麻疹 疫苗接种 的出生加权世界休息点为 78%,低于 92%–94% 群体范围;74% 的国家/地区休息点低于该水平,2019 年冠状病毒病 (COVID-19) 时代的转变使休息点中位数降低了 3.3 个百分点。讨论 由于在复制协议下,留存相对于支付工具的杠杆等于 1/(1 − x * ),而在任何平滑、有界选择协议下,在最后一英里上会出现分歧,因此缩小差距是违约和留存问题,而不是价格问题。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
疫苗接种 是卫生经济学中典型的正外部性:因为每个 疫苗接种 都保护其他人,所以预计去中心化选择将停滞在群体免疫阈值以下。提供的证据并未报告该研究解决的具体科学或公共卫生问题。
设计与对象
本研究直接估计了影响国家 疫苗接种 覆盖范围的运动定律。所提供的证据并未报告具体的研究设计、人群或环境。
核心方法
我将异质家庭人口的接种或不接种决策建模为预期效用问题,其净收益随着疫苗接种份额的增加而下降,通过明确的修订方案将其汇总为选择 突变(复制子 - 变异子)方程,其中收益响应切换与卫生系统接触和损耗风险共存,并根据世界卫生组织/联合国儿童基金会(WHO/UNICEF)估计所得方程1980-2025 年 195 个国家的国家免疫覆盖率估计(WUENIC,2025 年修订版),采用联合国 队列研究 分母和世界银行收入和卫生系统能力控制。
主要结果
首先,在覆盖范围内部,动态以较强的线性恢复力为主,这意味着相关池的年换手率为15%–25%;那里的收益斜率在统计上很弱,并且内部数据无法区分制度流动和对收益不敏感的修正,因此估计的风险从上面限制了制度成分。其次,搭便车特征出现在它被识别的地方:在跨越整个覆盖范围的 Hib 推出中急剧变化,而在第一剂 DTP 裕度上较弱。
解释与创新
由于在复制协议下,留存相对于支付工具的杠杆等于 1/(1 − x * ),而在任何平滑、有界选择协议下,在最后一英里会出现分歧,因此缩小差距是违约和留存问题,而不是价格问题。
科研与公卫意义
每个国家的运动规律都有一个稳定的内部静止点;第一剂 麻疹 疫苗接种 的出生加权世界休息点为 78%,低于 92%–94% 群体范围;74% 的国家/地区休息点低于该水平,2019 年冠状病毒病 (COVID-19) 时代的转变使休息点中位数降低了 3.3 个百分点。
局限与证据强度
提供的证据并未报告研究的具体局限性或证据强度。

The herd immunity attractor: selection, drift, and the steady-state undersupply of vaccination in 195 countries, 1980–2025

Authors: Aras Yolusever
Original abstract (English)
Introduction Vaccination is the canonical positive externality in health economics: because each vaccination protects others, decentralized choice is predicted to stall below the herd immunity threshold. Yet little evidence exists on the law of motion that governs national coverage. This study estimates it directly. Methods I model the vaccinate-or-not decision of a heterogeneous household population as an expected-utility problem whose net payoff declines in the vaccinated share, aggregate it through an explicit revision protocol into a selection-mutation (replicator-mutator) equation in which payoff-responsive switching coexists with health-system contact and attrition hazards, and estimate the resulting equation on the World Health Organization/United Nations Children's Fund (WHO/UNICEF) Estimates of National Immunization Coverage (WUENIC, 2025 revision) for 195 countries over 1980-2025, with UN cohort denominators and World Bank income and health-system capacity controls. An exact identification result shows that the linear turnover coefficient equals the difference between the law of motion at full and at zero coverage, so it is identified from boundary observations independently of the payoff parameters, whereas the payoff slope is identified only where coverage traverses the simplex. Results First, in the interior of the coverage range, the dynamics are dominated by a strong linear restoring force, implying an annual turnover of 15%–25% of the relevant pool; the payoff slope is statistically weak there, and interior data cannot distinguish institutional flows from payoff-insensitive revision, so the estimated hazards bound the institutional component from above. Second, the free-riding signature appears where it is identified: sharply in the Hib rollout, which spans the full coverage range, and weakly on the first-dose DTP margin. Third, every country's law of motion has a stable interior rest point; the births-weighted world rest point for first-dose measles vaccination is 78%, below the 92%–94% herd band; 74% of countries rest below it, and a coronavirus disease 2019 (COVID-19) era shift lowered the median rest point by 3.3 points. Discussion Because the leverage of retention over payoff instruments equals 1/(1 − x * ) under the replicator protocol and diverges along the last mile under any smooth, bounded choice protocol, closing the gap is a default-and-retention problem rather than a price problem.

Question and background
Vaccination is the canonical positive externality in health economics: because each vaccination protects others, decentralized choice is predicted to stall below the herd immunity threshold. The supplied evidence does not report the specific scientific or public-health problem addressed by the study.
Design and population
This study estimates the law of motion governing national vaccination coverage directly. The supplied evidence does not report the specific study design, population or setting.
Core methods
I model the vaccinate-or-not decision of a heterogeneous household population as an expected-utility problem whose net payoff declines in the vaccinated share, aggregate it through an explicit revision protocol into a selection-mutation (replicator-mutator) equation in which payoff-responsive switching coexists with health-system contact and attrition hazards, and estimate the resulting equation on the World Health Organization/United Nations Children's Fund (WHO/UNICEF) Estimates of National Immunization Coverage (WUENIC, 2025 revision) for 195 countries over 1980-2025, with UN cohort denominators and World Bank income and health-system capacity controls.
Main results
First, in the interior of the coverage range, the dynamics are dominated by a strong linear restoring force, implying an annual turnover of 15%–25% of the relevant pool; the payoff slope is statistically weak there, and interior data cannot distinguish institutional flows from payoff-insensitive revision, so the estimated hazards bound the institutional component from above. Second, the free-riding signature appears where it is identified: sharply in the Hib rollout, which spans the full coverage range, and weakly on the first-dose DTP margin.
Interpretation and novelty
Because the leverage of retention over payoff instruments equals 1/(1 − x * ) under the replicator protocol and diverges along the last mile under any smooth, bounded choice protocol, closing the gap is a default-and-retention problem rather than a price problem.
Scientific and public-health significance
Every country's law of motion has a stable interior rest point; the births-weighted world rest point for first-dose measles vaccination is 78%, below the 92%–94% herd band; 74% of countries rest below it, and a coronavirus disease 2019 (COVID-19) era shift lowered the median rest point by 3.3 points.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report the specific limitations or evidence strength of the study.

公共卫生药理学:人口水平免疫监测 (PLIM) 框架,以优化 疫苗 在非洲的表现

Public Health Pharmacology: a framework for population-level immunologic monitoring (PLIM) to optimize vaccine performance in Africa
作者： Adedeji A, Mohammed ElHabibi Adam E, Adegboyega T, Bale I, Adeyemo R, Gbadamosi M, Sibomana H, Abass K, Toure D
摘要中文翻译该循环分四个阶段进行: (A) Sentinel 输入利用可行的采样方法,例如来自代表性地区 队列研究 的干血斑 (DBS);(B) 药理学过滤器根据生物风险(例如遗传多态性 (HLA)、环境干扰 (NTM) 和合并感染)对这些数据进行分层;(C) 计划输出产生可操作的情报(例如,加强活动或配方更新);(D) 系统集成将这些见解反馈到国家免疫计划中,以优化未来的交付。信息图说明了循环免疫计划工作流程,其中包含四个标记部分:(A) Sentinel 输入从 队列研究 区收集干血点;(B) 药理学过滤器分析遗传、环境和合并感染因素;(C) 计划性产出提供有针对性的干预措施,例如加强活动、配方更新和冷链整顿;(D) 系统集成将见解反馈给国家免疫计划,以优化交付和保护。支柱 1:哨点免疫原性监测 为了最大限度地减少资源有限的卫生系统的后勤负担,PLIM 放弃了普遍检测。尽管撒哈拉以南非洲地区的行政免疫覆盖率很高,但意外的传染病 暴发 仍构成重大的公共卫生挑战。传统的跟踪严重依赖于后勤输入和反应性 监测,在现实世界的 宿主 免疫中留下了不受监控的生物缺口。本文提出了一种新颖的群体水平免疫监测(PLIM)系统设计概念框架,作为公共卫生药理学(PHP)的核心操作工具,以系统地弥合这一差距。PLIM 的运作涵盖三个相互关联的支柱:(1) 生物发送...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本文解决了需要一种系统方法来弥合 疫苗 覆盖范围和公共卫生临床结果之间的差距,提出一个人口水平免疫监测 (PLIM) 框架,以优化 疫苗 在非洲的表现。
设计与对象
该研究描述了卢旺达的前瞻性“探路者试点”验证路线图,采用了跨三个生态和卫生系统阶层的分层有目的抽样设计:高密度城市环境、农村流行地区和农业气候热应激区。抽样包括参加常规扩大免疫计划 (EPI) 时间表的婴儿和产前护理就诊期间的孕妇。
核心方法
抽样以双重节奏进行,结合了快速、滚动横截面每两年一次的审计和前瞻性纵向 队列研究 跟踪。免疫学数据点直接集成到国家健康信息系统(例如 DHIS2 跟踪器)中,并纳入环境指标来研究生物相互作用。
主要结果
本文概述了 PLIM 的概念框架,强调其在系统地弥合 疫苗 覆盖范围和临床结果之间差距方面的作用。它强调了免疫数据与卫生系统的整合以及哨兵 监测 模型的使用。
解释与创新
作者将 PLIM 解释为一种新颖的系统设计框架,它提供了缺失的生物学基线来解释 疫苗 的成功或失败。他们认为,它的作用是主动的、前瞻性的药效学审核,而不是回顾性评估。
科研与公卫意义
PLIM 可以通过提供结构化平台来调查影响 疫苗 反应的生物相互作用,从而为 监测、诊断 和风险评估提供信息。它使卫生系统能够区分程序性失败和生物性失败,从而加强有针对性的干预措施。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于卢旺达的概念框架和前瞻性“探路者试点”验证路线图,可能无法推广到其他地区。证据是温和的,因为它是基于提议的设计而不是经验结果。

Public Health Pharmacology: a framework for population-level immunologic monitoring (PLIM) to optimize vaccine performance in Africa

Authors: Adedeji A, Mohammed ElHabibi Adam E, Adegboyega T, Bale I, Adeyemo R, Gbadamosi M, Sibomana H, Abass K, Toure D
Original abstract (English)
The cycle proceeds in four stages: (A) Sentinel Input utilizes feasible sampling methods like Dried Blood Spots (DBS) from representative district cohorts; (B) The Pharmacological Filter stratifies this data against biological risks such as genetic polymorphisms (HLA), environmental interference (NTM), and co-infections; (C) Programmatic Output generates actionable intelligence (e.g., booster campaigns or formulation updates); and (D) System Integration feeds these insights back into the National Immunization Program to optimize future delivery.Infographic illustrating a cyclical immunization program workflow with four labeled sections: (A) Sentinel Input collects dried blood spots from district cohorts; (B) Pharmacological Filter analyzes genetic, environmental, and co-infection factors; (C) Programmatic Output delivers targeted interventions like booster campaigns, formulation updates, and cold-chain rectification; (D) System Integration feeds insights back into the National Immunization Program to optimize delivery and protection.Pillar 1: sentinel immunogenicity surveillanceTo minimize the logistical burden on resource-limited health systems, PLIM moves away from universal testing. Despite high administrative immunization coverage across Sub-Saharan Africa, unexpected infectious disease outbreaks pose significant public health challenges. Traditional tracking relies heavily on logistical inputs and reactive surveillance, leaving an unmonitored biological gap in real-world host immunity. This paper presents a novel systems-design conceptual framework for Population-Level Immunologic Monitoring (PLIM) as a core operational tool of Public Health Pharmacology (PHP) to systematically bridge this gap. PLIM operates across three interconnected pillars: (1) biological sent...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This paper addresses the need for a systematic approach to bridge the gap between vaccine coverage and clinical outcomes in public health, proposing a framework for Population-Level Immunologic Monitoring (PLIM) to optimize vaccine performance in Africa.
Design and population
The study describes a prospective "Pathfinder Pilot" validation roadmap in Rwanda, employing a stratified purposive sampling design across three ecological and health-system strata: high-density urban settings, rural endemic regions, and agro-climatic thermal stress zones. Sampling includes infants presenting for routine Expanded Programme on Immunization (EPI) schedules and pregnant women during antenatal care visits.
Core methods
Sampling operates on a dual cadence, combining rapid, rolling cross-sectional bi-annual audits and prospective longitudinal cohort tracking. Immunologic data points are integrated directly into national health information systems, such as the DHIS2 Tracker, and environmental metrics are incorporated to investigate biological interactions.
Main results
The paper outlines a conceptual framework for PLIM, emphasizing its role in systematically bridging the gap between vaccine coverage and clinical outcomes. It highlights the integration of immunologic data into health systems and the use of sentinel surveillance models.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret PLIM as a novel systems-design framework that provides a missing biological baseline to explain vaccine success or failure. They argue that it functions as an active, prospective pharmacodynamic audit rather than a retrospective evaluation.
Scientific and public-health significance
PLIM can inform surveillance, diagnosis, and risk assessment by offering a structured platform to investigate biological interactions affecting vaccine response. It enables health systems to distinguish between programmatic and biological failures, enhancing targeted interventions.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to a conceptual framework and a prospective "Pathfinder Pilot" validation roadmap in Rwanda, which may not be generalizable to other regions. The evidence is moderate as it is based on a proposed design rather than empirical results.

基于深度学习分类模型的痘痘皮损图像识别

Identification of Mpox Skin Lesion Images Based on Deep Learning Classification Models
作者： Xianghong Deng, Cheng Li, Zhiwei Zhou, Shuang Yang
摘要中文翻译Mpox(以前称为猴痘)是一种罕见的人畜共患疾病,可引起皮肤损伤,并有可能在全球传播。随着计算机科学与医学的不断深入融合,基于深度学习和神经网络的智能疾病检测模型已成为增强临床诊断和治疗的重要辅助工具。本研究对主流深度学习分类模型VGG19、ResNet50、DenseNet201、GoogLeNet、ConvNeXt_B在mpox皮肤图像识别任务中的性能进行了对比分析。对于二元分类任务,图像被分为“Mpox”类和“其他”类,其中后者包括水痘和 麻疹 病例,因为它们最初的皮疹和脓疱表现与 MPOX 非常相似。除了定量性能评估之外,本研究还进一步利用 GradCAM++ 生成类激活热图,提供模型决策的可视化可解释性。由此产生的热图通过突出显示关键病变区域来提供重要的临床诊断价值,从而提高模型透明度并支持临床医生验证预测。实验结果表明,在相同的模型参数和学习率下,ResNet50模型取得了更高的分类精度和推理速度,展示了其在mpox等疾病图像识别任务中精确诊断的应用潜力。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在通过开发用于mpox皮肤病变识别的深度学习模型来增强临床麻疹,解决区分mpox与其他类似疾病(如水痘和麻疹)的挑战。
设计与对象
该研究比较了主流深度学习分类模型,包括VGG19、ResNet50、DenseNet201、GoogLeNet和ConvNeXt_B在mpox皮肤图像识别任务中的性能。它涉及一个二元分类任务,其中图像被分类为“Mpox”类和“其他”类,其中包括水痘和 麻疹 病例。
核心方法
该研究采用深度学习分类模型来识别 MPOX 皮肤图像,重点是评估其性能。它还使用 GradCAM++ 生成类激活热图,提供模型决策的可视化可解释性。
主要结果
研究发现,在相同的模型参数和学习率下,ResNet50模型相比其他模型取得了更高的分类精度和推理速度。
解释与创新
作者将结果解释为证明了 ResNet50 在 MPOX 和其他疾病图像识别任务中实现准确 诊断 的潜力,突显了其在准确性和速度方面的卓越性能。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为开发更有效的MPOX诊断工具提供信息,帮助早期检测和改善临床决策过程。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于抽象级别的证据,没有提供详细的实验结果或统计分析,这可能会影响结论的强度。

Identification of Mpox Skin Lesion Images Based on Deep Learning Classification Models

Authors: Xianghong Deng, Cheng Li, Zhiwei Zhou, Shuang Yang
Original abstract (English)
Mpox (formerly known as monkeypox) is a rare zoonotic disease that can cause skin lesions and has the potential for global spread. With the deepening integration of computer science and medicine, intelligent disease detection models based on deep learning and neural networks have become important auxiliary tools for enhancing clinical diagnosis and treatment. This study conducted a comparative analysis of the performance of mainstream deep learning classification models,VGG19, ResNet50, DenseNet201, GoogLeNet, and ConvNeXt_B,in the task of mpox skin image recognition. For the binary classification task, images were categorized into a “Mpox” class and an “Others” class, where the latter includes chickenpox and measles cases, given that their initial rash and pustule presentations closely resemble those of mpox. Beyond quantitative performance evaluation, this study further employed GradCAM++ to generate class activation heatmaps, providing visual interpretability of model decision-making. The resulting heatmaps offer critical clinical diagnostic value by highlighting key lesion regions, thereby enhancing model transparency and supporting clinicians in validating predictions. The experimental results indicate that under identical model parameters and learning rates, the ResNet50 model achieved higher classification accuracy and inference speed, demonstrating its application potential for accurate diagnosis in mpox and other disease image recognition tasks.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to enhance clinical diagnosis by developing deep learning models for mpox skin lesion recognition, addressing the challenge of distinguishing mpox from other similar diseases like chickenpox and measles.
Design and population
The study compared the performance of mainstream deep learning classification models, including VGG19, ResNet50, DenseNet201, GoogLeNet, and ConvNeXt_B, in the task of mpox skin image recognition. It involved a binary classification task where images were categorized into a “Mpox” class and an “Others” class, which includes chickenpox and measles cases.
Core methods
The study employed deep learning classification models to recognize mpox skin images, with a focus on evaluating their performance. It also used GradCAM++ to generate class activation heatmaps, providing visual interpretability of model decision-making.
Main results
The study found that under identical model parameters and learning rates, the ResNet50 model achieved higher classification accuracy and inference speed compared to other models.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the results as demonstrating the potential of ResNet50 for accurate diagnosis in mpox and other disease image recognition tasks, highlighting its superior performance in terms of accuracy and speed.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the development of more effective diagnostic tools for mpox, aiding in early detection and improving clinical decision-making processes.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to abstract-level evidence and does not provide detailed experimental results or statistical analyses, which may affect the strength of the conclusions.

专门针对父亲健康的产前咨询是否可以扩大男性获得预防和护理的机会?

Does a prenatal consultation dedicated to fathers’ health widen men’s access to prevention and care?
作者： Pauline Penot, Gaëlle Jacob, Audrey Guerizec, Valérie-Anne Letembet, Raya Harich, Anne Simon, Miguel de Sousa Mendes, Pierre-Etienne Manuellan, Bruno Renevier, Yazdan Yazdanpanah
摘要中文翻译产前护理为准妈妈们提供了定期的医疗保健服务,但对于准爸爸们却没有同等的预防框架。PARAGE 项目评估了针对未来父亲的专门产前咨询,旨在维护和改善健康。我们描述本次咨询的临床结果并确定与这些结果相关的因素。PARAGE 是一项没有控制臂的单中心介入研究,在巴黎东北郊移民盛行的地区的一个大型公共产科病房启动。符合条件的孕妇被要求提供其伴侣的联系方式。在成功联系到的2516名符合条件的未来父亲中,有1333人被纳入其中;另外十四人直接寻求磋商。医生或助产士进行医疗访谈,立即进行生物测试,进行 疫苗接种 补习,并根据参与者的需求提供医疗保健和社会转介。父亲产前咨询带来了预防、诊断 和与护理的联系:37% 的参与者从未接受过艾滋病毒检测;96% 的人在当前怀孕期间或之前没有接受过检测,其中只有 1% 拒绝进行 HIV 筛查;72% 的人已经过时了百日咳或 麻疹 疫苗接种,其中 60% 的人接受了现场补补。此外,18% 的人至少诊断出一种病症或将其重新送回护理机构;17% 被转介给医疗保健专业人员,11% 被转介给社会工作者。在多变量分析中,出生在撒哈拉以南非洲而不是法国,没有健康保险而不是完整的医疗保险与医疗 诊断(分别为 OR 2.67 [1.80-3.98] 和 OR 2.58 [1.63-4.09])以及转诊至医疗保健专业人员(OR 2.67 [1.80-3.98] 和 OR 3.63)相关分别为[2.27-5.80])。PARAGE 导致了整个研究人群的 HIV 筛查和 疫苗接种 追赶。它还使未发现的疾病得以诊断,并恢复对已诊断但之前未能跟进的疾病的护理,特别是对于社会地位最不利的未来父亲。解决男性伴侣怀孕期间的健康问题可能有助于减少健康方面的性别和社会不平等。NCT05085717;注册处:ClinicalTrials.gov,美国国家医学图书馆 https://classic.clinicaltrials.gov/ct2/show/NCT05085717(追溯注册)。注册日期:2021 年 10 月 20 日。协议可作为附加文件 1 获取。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估针对准父亲的专门产前咨询,以改善健康结果并解决健康差异。对母亲的产前护理很完善,但对父亲却没有同等的框架。
设计与对象
PARAGE 项目是在巴黎东北郊的一个大型公共产房进行的一项单中心介入研究,重点关注了 1333 名被联系的未来父亲以及 14 名直接寻求咨询的人。
核心方法
该研究涉及医学访谈、规定的生物测试、疫苗接种 以及根据参与者的需求转介至医疗保健和社会服务。
主要结果
在参与者中,37% 的人从未接受过 HIV 检测,96% 的人在当前怀孕期间或之前没有接受过检测,72% 的人未接受过百日咳或 麻疹 麻疹 疫苗接种 的检测,60% 的人接受了补充 疫苗接种。此外,18% 的人患有未确诊的病症,17% 的人被转介给医疗保健专业人员,11% 的人被转介给社会工作者。
解释与创新
研究发现,父亲的产前咨询可以起到显着的预防作用,并与护理挂钩,特别是对于社会弱势男性。出生在撒哈拉以南非洲地区或没有健康保险与较高的医疗 诊断 率和转诊至医疗保健专业人员有关。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,解决男性伴侣怀孕期间的健康问题有助于减少健康方面的性别和社会不平等,特别是在移民较多的地区。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其单中心设计和缺乏对照组而受到限制,这可能会影响普遍性。结果基于观察数据,可能会受到混杂因素的影响。

Does a prenatal consultation dedicated to fathers’ health widen men’s access to prevention and care?

Authors: Pauline Penot, Gaëlle Jacob, Audrey Guerizec, Valérie-Anne Letembet, Raya Harich, Anne Simon, Miguel de Sousa Mendes, Pierre-Etienne Manuellan, Bruno Renevier, Yazdan Yazdanpanah
Original abstract (English)
Prenatal care provides expectant mothers with regular healthcare access, yet no equivalent preventative framework exists for expectant fathers. The PARTAGE project evaluated a dedicated prenatal consultation for future fathers aimed at health preservation and improvement. We describe the clinical outcomes of this consultation and identify factors associated with these outcomes. PARTAGE was a monocentric interventional study without control arm launched in a large public maternity ward in a northeastern suburb of Paris, an area where immigration prevails. Eligible pregnant women were offered to give their partner contact details. Among 2516 eligible future fathers who were successfully contacted, 1333 were included; fourteen others sought the consultation directly. A physician or a midwife held a medical interview, prescribed immediate biological tests, administered vaccination catch-up, and offered healthcare and social referral depending on participants' needs. Paternal prenatal consultations resulted in prevention, diagnosis and linkage into care: 37% of participants had never been tested for HIV; 96% had not been tested during or immediately prior to the current pregnancy, of whom only 1% refused HIV screening; 72% were out-of-date for pertussis or measles vaccinations, of whom 60% received an on-site catch-up. In addition, 18% had at least one pathology diagnosed or brought back into care; 17% were referred to healthcare professionals and 11% to social workers. In multivariate analysis, being born in Sub Saharan Africa rather than in France and having no health insurance coverage rather than a complete one were associated with a medical diagnosis (OR 2.67 [1.80-3.98] and OR 2.58 [1.63-4.09], respectively) and with a referral to a healthcare professional (OR 2.67 [1.80-3.98] and OR 3.63 [2.27-5.80], respectively). PARTAGE led to HIV screening and vaccinations catch-up across the whole study population. It also enabled the diagnosis of undetected illnesses and to the resumption of care for pathologies that had already been diagnosed but were previously lost to follow-up, especially in the most socially disadvantaged future fathers. Addressing men’s health during their partner’s pregnancy may help reduce gender and social inequalities in health. NCT05085717; Registry: ClinicalTrials.gov, US National Library of Medicine https://classic.clinicaltrials.gov/ct2/show/NCT05085717 (retrospectively registered). Registration date: 20 October 2021. Protocol is available as additional file 1.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate a dedicated prenatal consultation for expectant fathers to improve health outcomes and address health disparities. Prenatal care for mothers is well-established, but no equivalent framework exists for fathers.
Design and population
The PARTAGE project was a monocentric interventional study in a large public maternity ward in a northeastern suburb of Paris, focusing on 1333 future fathers who were contacted and 14 who sought the consultation directly.
Core methods
The study involved a medical interview, prescribed biological tests, vaccinations, and referrals to healthcare and social services based on participants' needs.
Main results
Among participants, 37% had never been tested for HIV, 96% had not been tested during or immediately prior to the current pregnancy, and 72% were out-of-date for pertussis or measles vaccinations, with 60% receiving catch-up vaccinations. Additionally, 18% had undiagnosed pathologies, 17% were referred to healthcare professionals, and 11% to social workers.
Interpretation and novelty
The study found that paternal prenatal consultations led to significant prevention, diagnosis, and linkage into care, particularly for socially disadvantaged men. Being born in Sub-Saharan Africa or having no health insurance coverage was associated with higher rates of medical diagnosis and referral to healthcare professionals.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that addressing men’s health during their partner’s pregnancy can help reduce gender and social inequalities in health, particularly in areas with high immigration.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its monocentric design and lack of a control group, which may affect generalizability. The results are based on observational data and may be subject to confounding factors.

全球 疫苗接种 计划对弹性的需求

The need for resilience in global vaccination programs
作者： Amy K. Winter, Matthew Joseph Ferrari, Winter AK, Ferrari MJ., Matthew J. Ferrari
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究强调了 麻疹 和百日咳的全球负担,强调需要建立更具弹性和可持续的 麻疹 计划,以应对疾病预防方面持续存在的挑战。
设计与对象
该研究回顾了有关 麻疹 和百日咳趋势的现有文献和数据,重点关注全球 麻疹 计划及其在减少疾病 发病率 方面的有效性。
核心方法
这些方法涉及分析来自全球研究和报告的 麻疹 和百日咳病例的趋势,包括来自 GBD 2023 百日咳合作者和 GBD 2023 麻疹 合作者的数据,以及对世界卫生组织和其他相关组织的参考。
主要结果
结果表明,麻疹 和百日咳仍然是重大的公共卫生挑战,最近的数据显示,麻疹 计划在减轻疾病负担方面有效,但很容易受到干扰,这凸显了恢复能力的必要性。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为加强全球 疫苗接种 计划的行动呼吁,并指出虽然取得了进展,但疾病复发的威胁仍然存在,这项研究增加了越来越多的证据支持弹性系统的需求。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为制定更具弹性和可持续性的 疫苗接种 计划提供了信息,这对于维持疾病控制和预防弱势群体中的 暴发 至关重要。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于现有文献和数据,在完整性和及时性方面可能存在局限性,但由于依赖全面的全球数据集和专家分析,证据有力。

The need for resilience in global vaccination programs

Authors: Amy K. Winter, Matthew Joseph Ferrari, Winter AK, Ferrari MJ., Matthew J. Ferrari
Original abstract (English)
New studies of trends in the global burden of measles and pertussis offer a reminder of how much vaccination has achieved and a warning of the need to build more resilient and sustainable vaccination programs. Question and background
The study highlights the global burden of measles and pertussis, emphasizing the need to build more resilient and sustainable vaccination programs to address ongoing challenges in disease prevention.
Design and population
The study is a review of existing literature and data on measles and pertussis trends, focusing on global vaccination programs and their effectiveness in reducing disease incidence.
Core methods
The methods involve analyzing trends in measles and pertussis cases from global studies and reports, including data from the GBD 2023 Pertussis Collaborators and GBD 2023 Measles Collaborators, as well as references to the World Health Organization and other relevant organizations.
Main results
The results indicate that measles and pertussis remain significant public health challenges, with recent data showing that vaccination programs have been effective in reducing disease burden but are vulnerable to disruptions, underscoring the need for resilience.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the findings as a call to action for strengthening global vaccination programs, noting that while progress has been made, the threat of disease resurgence remains, and this study adds to the growing body of evidence supporting the need for resilient systems.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the development of more resilient and sustainable vaccination programs, which are critical for maintaining disease control and preventing outbreaks in vulnerable populations.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on existing literature and data, which may have limitations in terms of completeness and timeliness, but the evidence is strong due to its reliance on comprehensive global datasets and expert analysis.

预测不确定性控制计算机模型校准

Prediction Uncertainty Controlled Computer Model Calibration
作者： R. Byun, Dayeon Yoon, Won Chang
摘要中文翻译计算机模型校准通常用于预测,但贝叶斯肯尼迪-奥哈根 (KOH) 校准和基于摊销模拟的推理都不是为了控制校准模拟器的预测误差而构建的。当参数识别不力或数据模型差异指定错误时,由标称后验水平引起的模拟器输出带可能远离标称值。我们提出了一个直接针对预测性错误覆盖的两层框架。首先,条件流匹配模型学习校准参数的摊销后验,并在受到不同结构的高斯过程差异扰动的模拟器输出上进行训练。其次,保形风险控制将目标观察的后验抽签安排到嵌套候选集中,并选择其固定秩逐点带在合成校准样本上达到规定的时间平均错误覆盖率的最小候选集。在校准和目标观测的可互换性下,这些带控制了有限样本中的预期错误覆盖。在综合基准中,建议的频带宽度约为 KOH-MCMC 频带宽度的 20%,误覆盖率接近 10% 的目标。在根据 2010 年马拉维 麻疹 暴发 校准年龄结构 SEIR 模型时,它们再次变得更加尖锐且接近名义值,并且它们未能涵盖的周数与不对称的流行病下降一致,暴露了机械模型的结构性不足。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在通过提出一个直接控制预测错误覆盖的新框架来解决计算机模型现有校准方法的局限性。背景强调,当参数识别较弱或数据模型差异指定错误时,贝叶斯肯尼迪-奥哈根 (KOH) 校准和基于摊销的模拟推理都无法充分管理预测误差。
设计与对象
该研究涉及综合基准以及针对 2010 年马拉维 麻疹 暴发 的年龄结构 SEIR 模型的校准。它的重点是评估所提议的框架在这​​些特定背景下的性能。
核心方法
这些方法包括一个两层框架:首先,使用条件流匹配模型来学习校准参数的摊销后验,并在具有高斯过程差异的模拟器输出上进行训练。其次,应用保形风险控制将后验抽签安排到嵌套的候选集中,选择实现规定的时间平均错误覆盖的最小集。
主要结果
在综合基准中,建议的频带比 KOH-MCMC 频带窄约 20%,误覆盖率接近 10% 的目标。当应用于 2010 年马拉维 麻疹 麻疹 暴发 时,这些区间更加尖锐且接近名义值,尽管它们未能覆盖非对称流行病下降期间的某些周。
解释与创新
作者将他们的发现解释为证明他们的框架在可交换性假设下有效控制了有限样本中的预测错误覆盖。新颖之处在于通过两层方法直接针对预测性错误覆盖,提供比现有方法更高的精度。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现对于提高流行病学研究中使用的预测模型的准确性具有重要意义,特别是对于 麻疹 等疾病,精确的预测对于公共卫生干预措施至关重要。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于综合基准和单个现实世界的 麻疹 暴发,这可能会影响普遍性。证据是强有力的,因为该框架在模拟和经验数据上都得到了验证,显示出一致的性能。

Prediction Uncertainty Controlled Computer Model Calibration

Authors: R. Byun, Dayeon Yoon, Won Chang
Original abstract (English)
Computer-model calibration is usually undertaken in service of prediction, yet neither Bayesian Kennedy-O'Hagan (KOH) calibration nor amortized simulation-based inference is built to control the predictive error of the calibrated simulator. When parameters are weakly identified or the data-model discrepancy is misspecified, the simulator-output bands induced by a nominal posterior level can be far from nominal. We propose a two-layer framework that targets predictive miscoverage directly. First, a conditional flow-matching model learns an amortized posterior for the calibration parameters, trained on simulator outputs perturbed with Gaussian-process discrepancies of varying structure. Second, conformal risk control arranges posterior draws for the target observation into nested candidate sets and selects the smallest one whose fixed-rank pointwise bands attain a prescribed time-averaged miscoverage on a synthetic calibration sample. Under exchangeability of calibration and target observations, the bands control expected miscoverage in finite samples. In a synthetic benchmark, the proposed bands are roughly 20% the width of KOH-MCMC bands with miscoverage near the 10% target. In calibrating an age-structured SEIR model to the 2010 Malawi measles outbreak, they are again sharper and near-nominal, and the weeks they fail to cover coincide with the asymmetric epidemic decline, exposing structural inadequacy of the mechanistic model.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the limitations of existing calibration methods for computer models by proposing a new framework that directly controls predictive miscoverage. The background highlights that neither Bayesian Kennedy-O'Hagan (KOH) calibration nor amortized simulation-based inference adequately manages predictive error when parameters are weakly identified or data-model discrepancy is misspecified.
Design and population
The study involved a synthetic benchmark and the calibration of an age-structured SEIR model to the 2010 Malawi measles outbreak. It focused on evaluating the performance of the proposed framework in these specific contexts.
Core methods
The methods included a two-layer framework: first, a conditional flow-matching model was used to learn an amortized posterior for calibration parameters, trained on simulator outputs with Gaussian-process discrepancies. Second, conformal risk control was applied to arrange posterior draws into nested candidate sets, selecting the smallest set that achieves a prescribed time-averaged miscoverage.
Main results
In the synthetic benchmark, the proposed bands were approximately 20% narrower than KOH-MCMC bands, with miscoverage close to the 10% target. When applied to the 2010 Malawi measles outbreak, the bands were sharper and near-nominal, though they failed to cover certain weeks during the asymmetric epidemic decline.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret their findings as demonstrating that their framework effectively controls predictive miscoverage in finite samples under exchangeability assumptions. The novelty lies in directly targeting predictive miscoverage through a two-layer approach, offering improved precision over existing methods.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings have implications for improving the accuracy of predictive models used in epidemiological studies, particularly for diseases like measles where precise forecasting is critical for public health interventions.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to synthetic benchmarks and a single real-world measles outbreak, which may affect generalizability. The evidence is strong, as the framework was validated on both simulated and empirical data, showing consistent performance.
